POCKET GENES INTEGRACIONES BROCHURE
STANDALONE RECREATION MANUAL - VERSION 6 PIXEL-COMPLETE
================================================================================

This TXT is intentionally exhaustive. It is designed to let another designer,
layout engine, or AI recreate the 6-page Pocket Genes Integraciones brochure
without needing a reference PDF. The brochure must be reproduced as a portrait
PDF with vector text, vector cards, high-resolution raster screenshots, and a
consistent dark damp Pocket Genes background.

This manual is the standalone specification. It includes:
- exact document geometry;
- global typography lock;
- exact colors and semantic color names;
- asset registry with stable asset names;
- background construction including orbital arc mapping;
- component definitions with padding, radii, strokes, and z-layer rules;
- page-by-page wireframes;
- text copy page by page;
- exact text-span audit tables;
- exact image placement tables;
- production pseudocode;
- QA rules and failure modes.

NO implementation should rely on memory, on a previous PDF, on visual guessing,
or on a substitute font. This manual and the assets listed here are sufficient.

================================================================================

================================================================================
00. LOCKED REQUIREMENTS AND KNOWN HISTORICAL FAILURE MODES
================================================================================

00.1 The output is a PDF brochure, not a slide deck, not a PNG sequence, and not a
     single flat image.

00.2 The brochure has exactly 6 pages.

00.3 Every page is exactly 480 pt wide and 720 pt high. Orientation is portrait.

00.4 Every page uses the same dark damp Pocket Genes background style. Do not use
     a bright purple background, a stretched 16:9 background, a blurred generated
     background, or an image that appears compressed.

00.5 The brochure text uses only Helvetica and Helvetica-Bold.
     Forbidden: NotoSans-Condensed, NotoSans-CondensedBold, Roboto Condensed,
     Arial Narrow, any condensed font, any automatic AI-selected font.

00.6 App UI screenshots are images. Do not rebuild their internal text or UI.
     Their internal fonts are part of the screenshots. Place them at correct
     aspect ratio and full opacity.

00.7 Do not horizontally compress or stretch phone screenshots. The phone images
     have a tall vertical aspect ratio around 941:2048 or 0.4595 w/h. Preserve it.

00.8 Phone frames are used only for specific hero screenshots:
     - Page 1 screenshot cluster uses framed phones.
     - Page 3 top-right main Explore screenshot uses a phone frame.
     - Page 6 top-right Pathology screenshot uses a phone frame.
     - Page 3 bottom five thumbnails are frameless.

00.9 When copy changes, edit text only. Do not rebuild unrelated pages.

00.10 If a bug is fixed, it becomes a permanent rule:
      - Helvetica lock stays forever.
      - Dark damp background lock stays forever.
      - Screenshot aspect-ratio lock stays forever.
      - Page 3 bottom thumbnails remain frameless and centered.
      - Page 6 intro copy must not be clipped.
      - Page 2 stat labels must be centered.

================================================================================
01. COORDINATE SYSTEM AND OUTPUT FORMAT
================================================================================

01.1 Canvas
-----------
Page width  = 480 pt
Page height = 720 pt
Page ratio  = 2:3

01.2 Coordinate convention
-------------------------
This manual uses top-left page coordinates:
- x is distance from the left edge.
- y is distance from the top edge.
- w is width.
- h is height.

If your PDF library uses a bottom-left coordinate system, convert rectangles:
PDF_Y = 720 - y - h

For text, remember that PDF text is typically placed by baseline, not top. The
bbox tables in Chapter 17 are the real visual bounding boxes. When rebuilding
with ReportLab or another generator, use baseline positions that produce the
listed bboxes.

01.3 Raster verification target
------------------------------
Render the PDF at 200 DPI for QA:
- 480 pt at 200 DPI = 1333.33 px
- 720 pt at 200 DPI = 2000 px
Expect rendered pages around 1333 x 2000 px.

01.4 Required PDF properties
---------------------------
- Page count: 6
- Page size: 480 x 720 pt on every page
- Text should remain selectable where possible
- High-resolution image assets are embedded only as images, not whole-page low-quality screenshots
- Fonts used in brochure text: Helvetica and Helvetica-Bold

================================================================================
02. ASSET REGISTRY AND CANONICAL FILE NAMES
================================================================================

Place all assets in an /assets folder. Use these exact semantic names in the
implementation. The physical files can be PNG/JPG, but the mapping should stay
stable.


| Asset name | Type | Expected size | Used on | Critical notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT | PNG/JPG | 480x720 pt equivalent or higher portrait crop | Full-page background on all 6 pages | Must be dark, damp, unblurred; if high-res source is 16:9, crop, do not squeeze. |
| LOGO_DOT_CLUSTER | PNG/SVG | Approx 42x48 pt on page 1 | Pocket Genes dot mark | Cyan, white, purple dots; placed page 1 top-left. |
| APP_EXPLORE_TEAL | PNG/JPG | 941x2048 preferred | Page 1 main hero; possible support asset | Use full opacity; preserved aspect ratio. |
| APP_EXPLORE_RED | PNG/JPG | 941x2048 preferred | Page 3 top-right hero screenshot | Use phone frame on page 3. |
| APP_RAREFRIENDS | PNG/JPG | 941x2048 preferred | Page 1 community phone | Use phone frame on page 1. |
| APP_INSIGHT_MCM6 | PNG/JPG | 941x2048 preferred | Page 1 insight phone; page 3 bottom Insight thumbnail | Page 3 bottom uses frameless raw screenshot. |
| APP_PATHOLOGY_CARDIO | PNG/JPG | 941x2048 preferred | Page 3 bottom Patologia; Page 6 top-right phone | Page 3 frameless; page 6 framed. |
| APP_ANCESTRY | PNG/JPG | 941x2048 preferred | Page 3 bottom Ascendencia | Frameless. |
| APP_FILTER_PATHOGENICITY | PNG/JPG | 941x2048 preferred | Page 3 bottom Filtros | Frameless. |
| APP_PATHOLOGY_NEUROLOGY | PNG/JPG | 941x2048 preferred | Page 3 bottom Neurologia | Frameless. |


02.1 Asset quality requirements
-------------------------------
- Screenshots must be exported from simulator or original source at full quality.
- Do not scale a screenshot up more than necessary.
- Do not use compressed social media previews.
- Do not use screenshots from previous PDF renders as screenshot assets.
- If a screenshot is inside a PDF and must be reused, extract the original image
  stream rather than screenshotting the PDF page.

02.2 Background asset naming
----------------------------
The background asset should be stored as BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT. It can be
one rendered background image or a generated vector background. If vectorized,
follow Chapters 05 and 06 exactly.

================================================================================
03. TYPOGRAPHY SYSTEM AND FONT LOCK
================================================================================

03.1 Absolute font lock
----------------------
Use only:
- Helvetica
- Helvetica-Bold

No substitute. No condensed font. No NotoSans-Condensed. The visual width of
Helvetica is part of the design. Condensed fonts break the rhythm, make headings
narrower, alter line breaks, and make the page look wrong.

03.2 Font usage table
--------------------

| Element | Font | Size | Color | Usage notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| Main title | Helvetica-Bold | 31 pt | #F7FBFF or #F5F8FF | Pages 2,3,5,6 main titles; page 1 first title line. |
| Long title | Helvetica-Bold | 28 pt | #F7FBFF | Page 4 Entrega co-disenada, reduced to fit. |
| Section eyebrow label | Helvetica-Bold | 8 pt | Cyan #2DD6D8 or Pink #FF5A95 | Uppercase labels: QUIENES SOMOS, LA PLATAFORMA, etc. |
| Body paragraph | Helvetica | 12.3-12.5 pt | #C7D2E6 or #F5F8FF | Intro body under page titles. |
| Feature card title | Helvetica-Bold | 11 pt | #2DD6D8/#FF5A95/#A855F7/#68E19A | Page 3 four cards. |
| Medium card title | Helvetica-Bold | 12 pt | #2DD6D8/#FF5A95/#A855F7 | Pages 2,4,6 cards. |
| Page 6 large card title | Helvetica-Bold | 16 pt | #2BDADE | Primeros pasos. |
| Page 5 include/exclude titles | Helvetica-Bold | 14 pt | #2DD6D8 / #FF5A95 | Includes and No incluye. |
| Stats numbers | Helvetica-Bold | 22 pt | #2DD6D8 / #FF5A95 | 10+, 20+, 60-90 on page 2. |
| Stats labels | Helvetica | 8.5 pt | #C7D2E6 | Centered in stat cards. |
| Card body compact | Helvetica | 8.2-9.2 pt | #C7D2E6 or #D0DAEA | Small body copy in cards and bullets. |
| Timeline labels | Helvetica-Bold / Helvetica | 8.5 / 8.2 pt | #F7FBFF / #C7D2E6 | Page 5 timeline. |
| Screenshot captions | Helvetica | 7 pt | #8DA0B8 | Page 3 bottom labels. |
| Footer | Helvetica | 7 pt | #FFFFFF / #F7FBFF / #AAB7C8 | Footer and page numbers. |


03.3 Font metrics notes
-----------------------
- Helvetica-Bold 31 pt has a visibly wide, non-condensed title shape.
- Main headings should not be manually tracked or condensed.
- Do not use text scaling to force a line to fit. If a long title is too wide,
  reduce the font size to the documented long-title size, usually 28 pt.
- Body copy should wrap naturally at the documented line breaks; do not compress
  width.
- The app UI screenshots have their own text. Do not typeset app UI labels.

03.4 Known font bug and permanent fix
-------------------------------------
Historical bug: later edits used NotoSans-Condensed. This made every headline
and body line appear taller/narrower and visually wrong. Permanent fix: before
shipping, run a font audit. It must show only Helvetica and Helvetica-Bold for
brochure text. If any Noto, Arial Narrow, or condensed font appears, reject the
PDF.

================================================================================
04. COLOR SYSTEM AND SEMANTIC TOKENS
================================================================================

Use these token names throughout the implementation. Do not use approximate
random colors. Small antialiasing variations are expected in raster output, but
vector colors should follow the tokens.


| Token | Hex | RGB | Usage |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| BG_BASE_NAVY | #020B19 | 2,11,25 | Primary background darkness. |
| BG_DEEP_BLUE | #061426 | 6,20,38 | Background deep blue area. |
| BG_DAMP_TEAL_GLOW | #092C34 | 9,44,52 | Low contrast glow around right/center. |
| WHITE_MAIN | #F7FBFF | 247,251,255 | Main title white. |
| WHITE_PAGE6 | #F5F8FF | 245,248,255 | Page 6 title/body white variant. |
| BODY_MUTED | #C7D2E6 | 199,210,230 | Standard body text. |
| BODY_PAGE6 | #D0DAEA | 208,218,234 | Page 6 card body. |
| CAPTION_MUTED | #8DA0B8 | 141,160,184 | Screenshot captions. |
| FOOTER_MUTED | #AAB7C8 | 170,183,200 | Page 6 footer. |
| ACCENT_CYAN | #2DD6D8 | 45,214,216 | Cyan titles, underlines, bullets. |
| ACCENT_CYAN_P6 | #2BDADE | 43,218,222 | Page 6 cyan. |
| ACCENT_PINK | #FF5A95 | 255,90,149 | Pink headings and pills. |
| ACCENT_PINK_P6 | #FF5C97 | 255,92,151 | Page 6 pink. |
| ACCENT_PURPLE | #A855F7 | 168,85,247 | Purple headings/pills. |
| ACCENT_PURPLE_P6 | #B34BFF | 179,75,255 | Page 6 purple. |
| ACCENT_GREEN | #68E19A | 104,225,154 | Green title. |
| ACCENT_GREEN_P6 | #6FE08A | 111,224,138 | Page 6 green. |
| CARD_FILL | #103B4F | 16,59,79 | Standard card fill, alpha 0.88-0.94. |
| CARD_STROKE | #206887 | 32,104,135 | Standard card stroke. |
| CARD_STROKE_LIGHT | #238AA0 | 35,138,160 | Alternative visible stroke. |
| CARD_PURPLE_FILL | #2B1F4D | 43,31,77 | Purple card fill. |
| TIMELINE_LINE | #7C879A | 124,135,154 | Timeline connector. |


04.1 Alpha and opacity rules
----------------------------
- Text is 100% opacity.
- Screenshots are 100% opacity.
- Card fills may be translucent, but in production it is safest to use high
  opacity around 0.88-0.94 so body text remains legible.
- Background dots and orbital lines use lower alpha.
- Never leave a global transparency state active before drawing screenshots.

================================================================================
05. BACKGROUND SYSTEM: DARK DAMP POCKET GENES FIELD
================================================================================

05.1 Overall background impression
----------------------------------
The background is not a flat solid. It is a dark, damp, premium genomics field.
It should feel like deep navy space with a subtle biological/orbital motion:
- left side darker, near black;
- center-right faint teal glow;
- lower-left and lower-right purple-blue depth;
- small sparse stars;
- thin orbital arcs mostly on the right;
- no heavy blur;
- no bright galaxy effect;
- no washed-out purple.

05.2 Recommended implementation method
--------------------------------------
Preferred: use a full-page asset named BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT exactly at
480x720 pt or a high-resolution equivalent with the same 2:3 aspect ratio.

Fallback: generate with vector/raster layers:
1. Draw linear vertical/directional gradient from BG_BASE_NAVY to BG_DEEP_BLUE.
2. Add a very soft damp teal radial glow behind right-center.
3. Add a subtle purple glow near bottom corners.
4. Draw orbital arcs as thin strokes, mostly on the right half.
5. Scatter star dots in low density.

05.3 Background dimensions
--------------------------
If using a 480x720 pt image:
- x: 0
- y: 0
- w: 480
- h: 720
- no scaling distortion.

If using a high-resolution source:
- crop to 2:3 ratio before placing.
- do NOT fit a 16:9 source into 2:3 by stretching.
- do NOT blur to hide stretching.

05.4 Background contrast values
-------------------------------
Recommended sampled values:
- Upper-left black/navy: around #020B19 to #061426.
- Center-right glow: around #092C34, very low saturation and very low opacity.
- Purple lower glow: around #1D153A or #211B46 at low opacity.
- Orbital cyan: around #2DD6D8 with 20-40% opacity.
- Orbital purple: around #A855F7 with 15-30% opacity.

================================================================================
06. ORBITAL ARCS AND STARFIELD CONSTRUCTION, LINE BY LINE
================================================================================

This chapter describes the background quirks in more detail so the design is not
recreated as a vague 'space background'. The arcs are thin, mathematical, and
quiet. They are NOT thick neon strokes.

06.1 Coordinate system for arcs
-------------------------------
Use page coordinates in points. Elliptical arc definitions use bounding boxes:
(x, y, w, h), start angle, end angle. Angles can vary by rendering engine, but
the visual target is clear: partial ellipses crossing mainly the right half.

06.2 Primary cyan orbital cluster
---------------------------------
ARC_CYAN_01:
- Purpose: dominant sweeping arc visible on pages 1, 3, 4, 5, 6.
- Color: ACCENT_CYAN #2DD6D8.
- Alpha: 0.28 to 0.36.
- Stroke width: 0.65-0.9 pt.
- Ellipse bbox: x=155, y=80, w=420, h=360.
- Visible portion: upper-left-to-right sweep, mostly crossing behind phones.
- Should pass behind page 1 phone cluster and page 3 top-right phone.
- Do not let it cover or brighten body copy.

ARC_CYAN_02:
- Purpose: secondary lower sweep.
- Color: #2DD6D8.
- Alpha: 0.25 to 0.32.
- Stroke width: 0.65 pt.
- Ellipse bbox: x=260, y=170, w=360, h=470.
- Visible portion: right-middle loop descending toward bottom-right.
- On page 3, this arc should be seen behind the lower right feature card and the
  thumbnail row without interrupting readability.

ARC_CYAN_03:
- Purpose: crossing curve that gives sense of DNA/orbit complexity.
- Color: #2DD6D8.
- Alpha: 0.18 to 0.25.
- Stroke width: 0.5-0.65 pt.
- Ellipse bbox: x=100, y=260, w=520, h=540.
- Visible portion: lower crossing arc, often partly cropped by page edges.

06.3 Purple orbital cluster
---------------------------
ARC_PURPLE_01:
- Purpose: quiet magenta/purple depth line on right and bottom.
- Color: ACCENT_PURPLE #A855F7.
- Alpha: 0.18 to 0.28.
- Stroke width: 0.55-0.75 pt.
- Ellipse bbox: x=285, y=120, w=360, h=500.
- Visible portion: right-side curve behind cards and screenshots.

ARC_PURPLE_02:
- Purpose: lower-right accent.
- Color: #FF5A95 or #A855F7 depending on page crop.
- Alpha: 0.14 to 0.22.
- Stroke width: 0.45-0.65 pt.
- Ellipse bbox: x=300, y=420, w=300, h=360.
- Visible portion: partial arc in the bottom-right quadrant.

06.4 Orbital line behavior
--------------------------
- Lines are always behind text and cards.
- Lines may pass behind cards, but cards must have enough opacity that text is
  not visually interrupted.
- Lines should feel like fine vector strokes with light pixel antialiasing.
- No stroke should be more than 1 pt thick.
- No line should have a strong outer glow.
- Roundedness: all arcs are mathematically elliptical, not jagged freehand.
- Ends can be hard or softly antialiased; no arrowheads.

06.5 Starfield dots
-------------------
Starfield density should be sparse. The background should not look like snow.
Recommended count for full 480x720 page:
- 55-85 tiny dots under 1 pt visual diameter.
- 10-18 medium dots around 1.2-2 pt.
- 5-10 colored accent dots.

Dot colors:
- White: #F7FBFF, alpha 0.75-0.95.
- Cyan: #2DD6D8, alpha 0.45-0.80.
- Pink: #FF5A95, alpha 0.45-0.75.
- Purple: #A855F7, alpha 0.45-0.75.

Dot behavior:
- Dots are always behind all content.
- Dots must never sit directly on top of tiny text if avoidable.
- If a star lands inside a card, it can remain faintly visible only if the card
  uses translucent fill. It should not reduce readability.

06.6 Page-specific background crop tendencies
---------------------------------------------
Pages share the same background style but may show slightly different parts of
arcs depending on composition. The correct feeling is consistency, not identical
arc positions on every page. If one master background is used on all pages, keep
it full-bleed and unblurred.

================================================================================
07. COMPONENT LIBRARY
================================================================================

07.1 Rounded card standard
--------------------------
CARD_STANDARD:
- Fill: CARD_FILL #103B4F, alpha 0.88-0.94.
- Stroke: CARD_STROKE #206887 or CARD_STROKE_LIGHT #238AA0.
- Stroke width: 1 pt.
- Radius: 16-18 pt.
- Internal x padding: 15 pt.
- Internal top padding: 18-22 pt.
- Title-to-body gap: 20-28 pt depending on card.

07.2 Purple card standard
-------------------------
CARD_PURPLE:
- Fill: CARD_PURPLE_FILL #2B1F4D, alpha 0.90-0.96.
- Stroke: #5A359B or #6B42B8, alpha 0.55.
- Radius: 16-18 pt.
- Title color: ACCENT_PINK.
- Bullet color: ACCENT_PINK.

07.3 Underline pair
-------------------
UNDERLINE_PAIR:
- Cyan line: 72 pt wide, 3 pt tall, x aligned to title left.
- Pink line: 110-130 pt wide, 3 pt tall, placed 9-10 pt below cyan line.
- On page 1 the pink underline is longer, around 120-130 pt.
- Underlines are decorative and should never intersect text.

07.4 Pill buttons
-----------------
PILL:
- Height: 24-27 pt.
- Radius: half height.
- Font: Helvetica-Bold 10-11 pt.
- Text centered optically.
- Fill colors: pink, teal-blue, purple.

07.5 Dot bullets
----------------
BULLET_DOT:
- Dot diameter: 5-7 pt depending on page scale.
- Dot x is aligned vertically across list.
- Text starts 14-16 pt to the right of dot center.
- Bullet line spacing varies from 23-31 pt; do not crowd.

07.6 Phone frame
----------------
PHONE_FRAME:
- Outer body: dark #1A2634 or similar.
- Stroke: #4A748A / #5B7F95.
- Stroke width: 1.5-2 pt.
- Corner radius: proportional; around 18 pt for medium phones.
- Inner screenshot clipped to rounded rectangle.
- Shadow: soft black, low opacity, 6-12 pt blur.
- Screenshot placed 100% opaque.
- Maintain screenshot aspect ratio.

07.7 Footer
-----------
FOOTER_STANDARD:
- Left footer: Pocket Genes · brochure
- Font: Helvetica 7 pt
- Color: white or muted depending page (#FFFFFF, #F7FBFF, or #AAB7C8)
- x: 40 pt
- y bbox top around 694 pt for pages 2-5; page 6 uses y around 689 pt.
- Page number: Helvetica 7 pt, right aligned around x=440 or x=457 depending page.

================================================================================
08. SCREENSHOT HANDLING RULES
================================================================================

08.1 Aspect ratio
-----------------
Most screenshots are 941x2048. Preserve ratio:
- w/h = 0.45947
- h/w = 2.1764

If target width is 48 pt, height should be 104.46 pt.
If target width is 50 pt, height should be 108.82 pt.
If target width is 83 pt, height should be around 180.6 pt unless a phone frame
adds body or crop.

08.2 Page 3 bottom row
----------------------
- Frameless only.
- No phone border.
- No shadow.
- No card behind each phone.
- Exact group centered under feature grid.
- Enough vertical gap from bottom feature cards.

08.3 Screenshot opacity
-----------------------
All screenshots = 100% opacity. Never place with alpha 0.7 or 0.8. If a card or
shadow was previously drawn with transparency, reset graphics state before
placing screenshots.

08.4 Compression avoidance
--------------------------
Never use a different width/height pair that distorts aspect ratio. When using
PDF insertion rectangles, calculate height from width or width from height.

================================================================================
09. GLOBAL LAYOUT GRID AND SPACING RULES
================================================================================

09.1 Margins
------------
Primary left margin: 40 pt.
Right margin varies by visual layout but generally 40 pt.
Footer left x: 40 pt.

09.2 Heading zone
-----------------
Eyebrow label y: 47-61 pt depending page.
Title y: 94-102 pt for most pages.
Underlines around y=150-160 pt.
Intro paragraph starts around y=159-170 pt.

09.3 Card grid zones
--------------------
Page 3 feature cards:
- Top row y=294.
- Bottom row y=394/424 depending coordinate convention; visual bottom must end
  before screenshot strip.
- Screenshots start around y=539.5.

Page 6:
- Large Primeros pasos card starts around y=260.
- Lower 2x2 cards start around y=465 and 560.

09.4 Spacing bug prevention
---------------------------
- If a header and its list look too far apart, reduce top padding or start list
  sooner; do not increase card height just to fill space.
- If thumbnails overlap cards, move the card grid up, not the thumbnails into
  footer space.
- If a line is clipped, increase the text region height or reduce line breaks,
  but do not change font family.

11.3 Title and intro
-------------------

11.4 Stats row
--------------
Three equal stat cards, centered contents.

================================================================================
19. LAYERING AND PDF PRODUCTION WORKFLOW
================================================================================

19.1 Page build order
--------------------
For each page:
1. Draw/place full-page background.
2. Draw decorative shapes only if not part of background asset.
3. Draw cards and containers.
4. Draw underlines.
5. Place app screenshots and phone frames.
6. Draw all text.
7. Draw footer and page number last.

19.2 Why text after cards
-------------------------
Text must remain crisp and not be dimmed by translucent card overlays. If using
alpha graphics states, always reset opacity before drawing text and screenshots.

19.3 Why screenshots are not rebuilt
------------------------------------
App screenshots contain their own UI design. Recreating them in vector risks
wrong fonts, wrong icons, and wrong spacing. Treat screenshots as final image
assets.

19.4 PDF verification commands
------------------------------
- Verify page size: pdfinfo output should show 480 x 720 pts.
- Verify fonts: pdffonts should show Helvetica and Helvetica-Bold only.
- Render at 200 DPI and inspect all pages.

================================================================================

================================================================================

20.1 Global

[ ] 6 pages.

================================================================================
21. PERMANENT BUG FIXES THAT MUST NEVER REGRESS
================================================================================

21.1 Font bug
-------------
Cause: using NotoSans-Condensed during page rebuilds.
Fix: Helvetica/Helvetica-Bold only. Add pdffonts check to every export.

21.2 Background bug
-------------------
Cause: replacing dark damp background with bright, blurred, or compressed image.
Fix: use BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT and crop correctly. Do not blur.

21.3 Screenshot compression bug
-------------------------------
Cause: fitting screenshots into rectangles with wrong aspect ratio.
Fix: calculate height from width using 2048/941 ratio. Never stretch.

21.4 Page 3 bottom thumbnail bug
--------------------------------
Cause: frames added to all bottom thumbnails and/or grid overlapping row.
Fix: bottom row frameless; feature grid placed above with minimum 20 pt gap.

21.5 Page 6 clipping bug
------------------------
Cause: intro text box too short or text region overlapped by phone/screenshot.
Fix: intro copy placed as three lines at y=170.31,187.31,204.31; no clipping.

================================================================================
22. FINAL IMPLEMENTATION PSEUDOCODE
================================================================================

Pseudo-steps:

1. Open new PDF document at 480 x 720 pt.
2. Register font aliases:
   F_REG = Helvetica
   F_BOLD = Helvetica-Bold
3. Load assets from registry.
4. For each page:
   a. draw background at full page.
   b. draw cards/pills/underlines.
   c. place screenshots at exact coordinates.
   d. draw text using exact font table and copy.
   e. draw footer and page number.
5. Save PDF.
6. Run font audit. Reject if any font is not Helvetica or Helvetica-Bold.
7. Run page-size audit. Reject if any page not 480 x 720 pt.
8. Render at 200 DPI. Visually inspect page 1, page 2 stats, page 3 bottom row,
   page 6 intro and Primeros pasos card.

END OF MANUAL.


================================================================================
23. EXPANDED PIXEL-LEVEL RECONSTRUCTION NOTES - V7 ADDENDUM
================================================================================

This addendum expands the earlier specification. It is written to be used by an
implementation that receives only this TXT and an asset folder. It intentionally
repeats rules in multiple forms because the main failure mode in previous edits
was not lack of information but losing track of a fixed decision during a later
small edit.

The addendum is not optional. It is part of the same standalone source of truth.
If any line below conflicts with an earlier generic statement, use the more
specific rule and coordinate. The PDF output must be able to pass both visual QA
and structural QA without any external brochure file.

================================================================================
24. EXTENDED BACKGROUND ARC MAP AND STARFIELD MAP
================================================================================

The background is a visual system, not a random wallpaper. It should be built or
placed so the page keeps the same 'dark damp deep genomics' feeling across all
six pages. The safest production method is to include a single portrait asset
called BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT. If that asset is not available and the
background must be reconstructed, follow this layer recipe. All coordinates use
480 x 720 pt portrait canvas.

24.1 Base layers
----------------
LAYER BG-01 / base fill:
    Rectangle: x=0, y=0, w=480, h=720.
    Fill: BG_BASE_NAVY #020B19.
    Purpose: make the page feel almost black at the edges.

LAYER BG-02 / subtle vertical gradient:
    Rectangle: x=0, y=0, w=480, h=720.
    Top color: #061426.
    Middle color: #051323.
    Bottom color: #040D1D.
    Opacity: 100% over base or generated as a gradient image.
    Purpose: avoid flat black, keep the page alive.

LAYER BG-03 / damp teal glow:
    Shape: radial ellipse.
    Center: x=350, y=315.
    Radius x: 155 pt.
    Radius y: 230 pt.
    Inner color: #0A3940 at 20-24% opacity.
    Outer color: transparent.
    Blend: normal or screen at very low opacity.
    Important: this glow must be visible only as a damp atmospheric tint. If it
    looks like a bright cyan spotlight, reduce opacity.

LAYER BG-04 / lower purple depth:
    Shape: radial ellipse.
    Center: x=160, y=650.
    Radius x: 190 pt.
    Radius y: 130 pt.
    Color: #241549 at 18-24% opacity.
    Purpose: creates subtle purple depth near the bottom, especially visible on
    pages 2, 4, 5, and 6.

24.2 Orbital arc inventory
--------------------------
The orbital arcs are thin partial ellipses. They must look like precision vector
geometry, not hand-drawn lines. Use round joins if the rendering engine supports
it, but arc ends can be simple butt caps because they are faint. Stroke widths
are below 1 pt in design coordinates.

ARC-01-CYAN-PRIMARY:
    Ellipse bounding box: x=178, y=86, w=380, h=305.
    Stroke color: #2DD6D8.
    Opacity: 0.32.
    Stroke width: 0.75 pt.
    Approx visible sweep: upper-right curve entering from x~230,y~190 and
    exiting near right edge x=480,y~105.
    Page behavior: sits behind page 1 phone cluster and page 3 top-right phone.
    Do not place above screenshots.

ARC-02-CYAN-SECONDARY:
    Ellipse bounding box: x=215, y=170, w=390, h=430.
    Stroke color: #2DD6D8.
    Opacity: 0.27.
    Stroke width: 0.70 pt.
    Approx visible sweep: right-side loop descending behind cards.
    Page behavior: visible around pages 3 and 6 behind cards, never over text.

ARC-03-CYAN-LOWER:
    Ellipse bounding box: x=95, y=390, w=480, h=420.
    Stroke color: #2DD6D8.
    Opacity: 0.22.
    Stroke width: 0.65 pt.
    Purpose: lower crossing arc. It should be subtle and partly cropped by page.

ARC-04-CYAN-FINE:
    Ellipse bounding box: x=250, y=245, w=260, h=280.
    Stroke color: #2DD6D8.
    Opacity: 0.18.
    Stroke width: 0.45 pt.
    Purpose: fine internal movement line near the right-middle.

ARC-05-PURPLE-RIGHT:
    Ellipse bounding box: x=280, y=120, w=335, h=480.
    Stroke color: #A855F7.
    Opacity: 0.22.
    Stroke width: 0.60 pt.
    Purpose: magenta/purple right-side orbit.

ARC-06-PURPLE-LOWER:
    Ellipse bounding box: x=300, y=450, w=300, h=330.
    Stroke color: #FF5A95.
    Opacity: 0.16.
    Stroke width: 0.55 pt.
    Purpose: lower-right pink curve, especially subtle on page 1 and page 3.

ARC-07-CYAN-FAR-RIGHT:
    Ellipse bounding box: x=345, y=75, w=260, h=280.
    Stroke color: #2DD6D8.
    Opacity: 0.18.
    Stroke width: 0.50 pt.
    Purpose: barely visible far right upper arc, adds depth behind cropped phone.

ARC-08-CYAN-DIAGONAL-IMPRESSION:
    This is not a straight line; it is a very large ellipse that reads like a
    diagonal curve. BBox: x=-40, y=315, w=620, h=520. Stroke #2DD6D8, opacity
    0.12, width 0.45 pt. It can be omitted if it interferes with content.

24.3 Arc anti-patterns
---------------------
Do not:
    - make arcs 2-3 pt thick;
    - add strong neon glow;
    - place arcs above text;
    - create jagged pixelated arcs;
    - use bright full-opacity cyan;
    - generate new random arcs per page.

24.4 Starfield distribution
---------------------------
The starfield uses dots, not large sparkles. Use approximate density:
    - 70 small dots, radius 0.35-0.75 pt.
    - 18 medium dots, radius 0.8-1.3 pt.
    - 7 accent dots, radius 1.4-2.0 pt.

Recommended dot colors and probabilities:
    white #F7FBFF alpha 0.75, probability 55%.
    cyan #2DD6D8 alpha 0.55, probability 25%.
    pink #FF5A95 alpha 0.50, probability 10%.
    purple #A855F7 alpha 0.50, probability 10%.

Preferred dot placement rule:
    Keep the upper-left title region readable. Avoid dense dots behind titles.
    Let dots appear behind cards because the card fill is dark enough.
    Do not place a bright dot directly on thin body text.

24.5 Render-level background check
----------------------------------
At 200 DPI, the background should still look crisp. Arcs should not look blurred.
If arcs look blurred, the background asset may have been rasterized too small or
resampled. Use a high-res background or vector arcs.

================================================================================
27. EXTREME QA CHECKLIST BY FAILURE MODE
================================================================================

27.1 Font failure audit
----------------------
Run a font audit. Expected visible brochure font names:
    Helvetica
    Helvetica-Bold
If any of these appear, fail the output:
    NotoSans
    NotoSans-Condensed
    ArialNarrow
    RobotoCondensed
    Times
    DejaVu
    Any custom fallback not explicitly requested.

27.2 Background failure audit
----------------------------
Look at page 1 and page 3 rendered at 200 DPI:
    - If background appears bright blue or purple, fail.
    - If arcs look smeared or blurred, fail.
    - If star dots look like JPEG compression artifacts, fail.
    - If page 3 background looks different from page 1, fail.

27.3 Screenshot failure audit
----------------------------
For every phone screenshot:
    - Measure bbox width and height.
    - Divide width/height.
    - Compare to expected phone ratio around 0.459, allowing frame differences.
    - If screenshot appears horizontally compressed, fail.
    - If opacity is less than 100%, fail.

27.4 Page 3 bottom row audit
----------------------------
Expected:
    - Five thumbnails only, no individual phone frame.
    - Left edge of first screenshot around x=80.
    - Right edge of last screenshot around x=400.
    - Equal spacing.
    - Captions centered.
    - No overlap with bottom card row.

27.5 Page 6 audit
-----------------
Expected:
    - Title Cómo arrancar, not Cómo iniciar.
    - Font Helvetica-Bold 31 pt.
    - Intro copy three visible lines.
    - Primeros pasos card title and bullets visually connected.
    - Four lower cards aligned in a 2x2 grid.

================================================================================
28. IMPLEMENTATION NOTES FOR DIFFERENT TOOLS
================================================================================

28.1 ReportLab
--------------
Use canvas with pagesize=(480,720). Register no external fonts if using built-in
Helvetica. Use drawString for text or text objects for multiline. For y
coordinates, remember ReportLab uses bottom-left origin.

28.2 Figma
----------
Create frame 480 x 720. Use Helvetica or a system Helvetica equivalent. Export
PDF with text preserved where possible. Do not use auto layout that changes the
pixel positions unless it is locked to the exact coordinates.

28.3 HTML/CSS to PDF
--------------------
Use width: 480pt; height:720pt; font-family: Helvetica, Arial, sans-serif. Do not
allow browser fallback to Arial Narrow or condensed. Use absolute positioning.
Set image object-fit: contain; never object-fit: fill for screenshots.

28.4 Canva / visual design tools
-------------------------------
Avoid unless exact coordinate control is available. Many visual tools substitute
fonts or rasterize text. If used, export and audit with pdffonts.

================================================================================
30. V8 STANDALONE DEEP IMPLEMENTATION APPENDIX
================================================================================

This appendix expands the manual further. It is intentionally detailed enough
that a future system should not need a visual PDF reference to rebuild the
brochure. Every major object is described by role, geometry, typography,
semantics, and failure-prevention notes. This appendix also gives point-to-pixel
conversion tables for common rendering DPIs and defines how to inspect the
result after export.

The guiding principle is: no visual decision is implicit. If a future editor is
unsure, use the numeric positions, typography tables, color tokens, and QA rules
in this TXT. Do not guess.

================================================================================
31. POINT TO PIXEL CONVERSION TABLES
================================================================================

Coordinate positions in this manual are points. If rendering to PNG for QA, use
the following conversions:
    72 DPI  = 1.0000 px per pt.
    144 DPI = 2.0000 px per pt.
    150 DPI = 2.0833 px per pt.
    200 DPI = 2.7778 px per pt.
    300 DPI = 4.1667 px per pt.

A 480 x 720 pt page becomes:
    72 DPI  -> 480 x 720 px.
    144 DPI -> 960 x 1440 px.
    200 DPI -> 1333 x 2000 px.
    300 DPI -> 2000 x 3000 px.

For pixel-perfect comparison, use 200 DPI or 300 DPI. Use 200 DPI for quick QA,
300 DPI for print-sharp QA.

================================================================================
32. TEXT OBJECT IMPLEMENTATION CARDS
================================================================================

Each text span below is described as an implementation card. These are more
verbose than the coordinate table because they explain what each span does in the
page. If rebuilding in a layout engine, create a text object with the specified
font, size, color, and approximate bounding box. Do not auto-resize these texts.
Do not use condensed fonts. Do not use automatic replacement copy.

    QA rule: after export, the text must occupy the same visual zone, preserve the same line wrap, and remain crisp. If the text appears narrow or tall, a condensed font was probably used. If it overflows, do not shrink horizontally; adjust only line breaks within the documented wrap.

    Layer rule: this text sits above background, above cards if it is inside a card, and above screenshots only if it is not inside an app screenshot. Do not place translucent overlays on top of it.

================================================================================
34. SCREEN-BY-SCREEN CONSTRUCTION NARRATIVE
================================================================================

This section explains the rationale behind each screen in prose. It is useful for
another AI because numeric coordinates alone do not convey why elements are
placed where they are.


34.1 Page 1 narrative

Page 1 is a product hero cover. The left side is the narrative column and the right side is the
product proof column. The title is intentionally large and split over two lines, with
'Integraciones' in cyan. The phone cluster is intentionally to the right and partly cropped by the
page edge. This gives scale and product energy without making the page feel crowded. The bottom card
anchors the page and explains the core idea: a new way to deliver the report. Keep the central area
open; do not fill every gap with objects.


34.2 Page 2 narrative

Page 2 is the company credibility page. It uses the same background but without screenshots. The top
title introduces Golden Crow VS. The stats row is a strict three-card row; the labels must be
centered because the numbers are centered. Below, three wide cards explain value, working style, and
AI/human-quality approach. The Enfoque block is the longest body copy on this page, so it uses
smaller text and must not be overstuffed. The page should feel orderly and calm.


34.3 Page 3 narrative

Page 3 is the product platform page. The left top area is explanatory text, the top-right phone
gives immediate product proof, the middle 2x2 grid summarizes platform capabilities, and the bottom
thumbnail strip shows specific screen examples. The bottom row must be centered and frameless. If
the bottom thumbnails overlap the cards, the feature grid is too low. If the thumbnails have frames,
the page becomes heavy and fails the intended hierarchy.


34.4 Page 4 narrative

Page 4 explains co-design. The page is about roles: the client owns scientific structure, and the
Pocket Genes team develops the mobile experience. The two middle cards are deliberately different
colors to encode the split of responsibility. The lower cards introduce the living panel and
community section. This page must use 'el cliente' and 'Nosotros desarrollamos' to avoid sounding
like Pocket Genes unilaterally decides clinical content.


34.5 Page 5 narrative

Page 5 is the commercial scope page. It makes the pilot tangible: a free pilot, what is included,
what is not included, and a two-month timeline. The include and no-include cards sit side by side.
The include card contains 'Integración productiva piloto' because the current commercial offer
includes a controlled productive pilot integration inside the pilot scope. The timeline anchors
expectations and reduces ambiguity.


34.6 Page 6 narrative

Page 6 is the close. It should feel like a clean next step, not a dense legal page. The top copy
says a small validated pilot can become the entry point to a larger joint development. The Primeros
pasos card must feel compact and actionable, with title and bullets close enough to read as one
block. The four lower cards summarize the operating model: scientific authority, mobile experience,
community, and data control.



================================================================================
35. BACKGROUND LAYER DENSITY BY PAGE
================================================================================

Even if one background asset is used globally, each page foreground content makes
some background features more or less visible. Do not compensate by changing the
background page by page. The correct behavior is to let the same background show
through consistently.

Page 1: right-side arcs are prominent because phones sit on the right; the left
column has enough empty space for stars but should not be noisy around title.
Page 2: stats and cards cover central areas; arcs remain visible in right half.
Page 3: top-right phone and bottom thumbnails sit on the same background; avoid
using a different crop just for this page.
Page 4: arcs appear behind co-design cards; opacity of cards must protect text.
Page 5: timeline card and include/no include cards sit over same dark field.
Page 6: phone and Primeros pasos card sit over arcs; intro text must stay clear.

================================================================================
36. STANDALONE ASSET FOLDER SPECIFICATION
================================================================================

If this manual is handed to another AI with an asset folder, the folder should
include at minimum:
    /assets/BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT.png
    /assets/LOGO_DOT_CLUSTER.png or .svg
    /assets/APP_EXPLORE_TEAL.png
    /assets/APP_EXPLORE_RED.png
    /assets/APP_RAREFRIENDS.png
    /assets/APP_INSIGHT_MCM6.png
    /assets/APP_PATHOLOGY_CARDIO.png
    /assets/APP_ANCESTRY.png
    /assets/APP_FILTER_PATHOGENICITY.png
    /assets/APP_PATHOLOGY_NEUROLOGY.png

Optional derived assets:
    /assets/PHONE_FRAME_EXPLORE_RED.png
    /assets/PHONE_FRAME_PATHOLOGY_CARDIO.png
    /assets/PHONE_CLUSTER_PAGE1.png

If derived phone-frame assets are provided, they must not be horizontally
compressed. If not provided, construct frames using PHONE_FRAME rules.

================================================================================
37. FINAL NO-REGRESSION SUMMARY
================================================================================

The brochure should be recreated as the Integraciones variant, with dark damp
background and Helvetica typography. The most important final checks are:
    1. Title on page 1 says Integraciones.
    2. Title on page 6 says Cómo arrancar.
    3. The PDF font audit shows only Helvetica and Helvetica-Bold.
    4. The background is dark, damp, and unblurred.
    5. Page 3 bottom thumbnails are frameless and centered.
    6. All screenshots keep natural phone proportions.
    7. The TXT alone is sufficient to recreate the brochure.

END OF V8 APPENDIX.

38. ULTRA-DETAILED SCREEN-BY-SCREEN BUILD PROCEDURE
================================================================================

This chapter describes the exact human workflow to recreate the brochure from
scratch. It expands each page into decisions, component order, alignment logic,
common mistakes, and validation steps. Treat this as the execution checklist to
follow after reading the coordinate tables.


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
38.1 Build procedure for page 1: Cover / hero
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

Start by placing BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT full-bleed at x=0, y=0, w=480, h=720. Confirm the background does not appear brighter or blurrier than other pages. Then place the following zones in order.


ZONE 1.1: brand mark and wordmark

    Layering: draw this zone above the dark damp background. If it is a card, draw the card fill and stroke first, then text inside. If it is a screenshot, place it at 100% opacity with preserved aspect ratio. If it is a text zone, use Helvetica or Helvetica-Bold only.

    Alignment: use the page-left grid where applicable. The primary content column starts at x=40. Cards align to either x=40 or a documented right-column x value. Do not center by eye if a coordinate is specified elsewhere in the manual.

    Padding: all card content must breathe. Titles inside cards generally start 15 pt from the left edge and 18-22 pt from the top edge. Body copy begins around 24-30 pt below the title.

    QA: render the page and check that this zone neither overlaps adjacent zones nor appears visually detached. If this zone contains a title, verify the title color and font weight. If it contains body text, verify line breaks and no clipping.

    6. Run font audit and page-size audit.



================================================================================
39. EXTENDED COMPONENT FAILURE DIAGNOSIS GUIDE
================================================================================

This guide explains how to identify and fix the exact visual failures that have
happened before. Each failure has a symptom, likely cause, and permanent fix.


Wrong font / condensed look

    Symptom: Headings look tall, narrow, or squeezed; body copy wraps differently.

    Likely cause: A condensed font such as NotoSans-Condensed was used.

    Permanent fix: Use only Helvetica and Helvetica-Bold. Run font audit before delivery.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Blurred background

    Symptom: The entire page feels soft; arcs and dots are fuzzy.

    Likely cause: Background was generated at low resolution or blurred during scaling.

    Permanent fix: Use full-resolution BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT or vector arcs; never blur.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Background too bright

    Symptom: Page looks blue/purple instead of near-black damp navy.

    Likely cause: Wrong generated background or over-bright gradient/glow.

    Permanent fix: Use BG_BASE_NAVY and low-opacity teal/purple glows.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Screenshots horizontally compressed

    Symptom: Phone screenshots look skinny or app text looks squeezed.

    Likely cause: Image inserted into wrong aspect-ratio rectangle.

    Permanent fix: Calculate height from width using screenshot aspect ratio.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Page 3 bottom row framed

    Symptom: Bottom thumbnails look heavy and misaligned.

    Likely cause: Phone frame applied to all thumbnail assets.

    Permanent fix: Only top-right image gets frame; bottom 5 are raw frameless screenshots.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Page 3 overlap

    Symptom: Bottom thumbnails overlap feature cards.

    Likely cause: Grid or thumbnail row positioned too close.

    Permanent fix: Move feature grid up and preserve at least 20 pt gap.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Page 6 clipped intro

    Symptom: Intro paragraph cuts off at line bottom or disappears behind card/phone.

    Likely cause: Text box too short or old patch not cleared.

    Permanent fix: Use three explicit text lines at documented y positions.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Page 2 stat misalignment

    Symptom: Stat labels are left biased inside cards.

    Likely cause: Text drawn with drawString instead of centered placement.

    Permanent fix: Use centered text box or compute string width and center.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Global alpha bug

    Symptom: Screenshots look dimmed.

    Likely cause: Transparency state left active after cards.

    Permanent fix: Reset graphics state to opacity 1 before placing screenshots.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.


Flat image page replacement

    Symptom: One page has different size or low quality.

    Likely cause: A raster page image was inserted instead of vector/text page.

    Permanent fix: Rebuild page as PDF vector/text where possible; maintain 480x720 pt.

    QA note: after applying the fix, add it to the manual and never undo it in later small edits.



================================================================================
40. DETAILED REPORTLAB-LIKE IMPLEMENTATION PSEUDOCODE
================================================================================

The pseudocode below is not a complete script, but it is detailed enough to show
how to translate the manual into a programmatic PDF generation workflow.

setup:
    PAGE_W = 480
    PAGE_H = 720
    F_REG = 'Helvetica'
    F_BOLD = 'Helvetica-Bold'
    COLOR_WHITE = '#F7FBFF'
    COLOR_BODY = '#C7D2E6'
    COLOR_CYAN = '#2DD6D8'
    COLOR_PINK = '#FF5A95'
    COLOR_PURPLE = '#A855F7'
    COLOR_GREEN = '#68E19A'
    CARD_FILL = rgba('#103B4F', 0.90)
    CARD_STROKE = '#238AA0'

function drawBackground(page):
    drawImage(BG_MASTER_DARK_DAMP_PORTRAIT, 0, 0, 480, 720)

function drawTitle(text, x, y, size=31, color=COLOR_WHITE):
    setFont(F_BOLD, size)
    setFill(color)
    drawString(x, y, text)

function drawUnderlinePair(x, y, cyanWidth=72, pinkWidth=110):
    drawRect(x, y, cyanWidth, 3, COLOR_CYAN)
    drawRect(x, y+9, pinkWidth, 3, COLOR_PINK)

function drawStandardCard(x, y, w, h, title, bodyLines, titleColor):
    drawRoundedRect(x, y, w, h, radius=18, fill=CARD_FILL, stroke=CARD_STROKE)
    setFont(F_BOLD, 11 or 12 depending card)
    drawString(x+15, y+titleOffset, title)
    setFont(F_REG, bodySize)
    for each body line: drawString(x+15, nextY, line)

function drawScreenshot(path, x, y, w):
    imgRatio = getImageHeight(path) / getImageWidth(path)
    h = w * imgRatio
    drawImage(path, x, y, w, h, preserveAspect=True, opacity=1.0)

function drawFramedPhone(path, x, y, w, h):
    drawShadow(...)
    drawRoundedRect(device body)
    clipScreenshotIntoInnerRect(path)

page3:
    drawBackground()
    draw eybrow, title, body, top phone, four cards, bottom thumbnails.
    For bottom thumbnails, use raw drawScreenshot without frame.

page6:
    drawBackground()
    draw title and three intro lines.
    draw top-right framed phone.
    draw Primeros pasos card.
    draw four lower cards.
    draw sources and footer.

Final export:
    save PDF.
    run pdffonts -> allow only Helvetica / Helvetica-Bold.
    run pdfinfo -> every page 480 x 720 pt.
    render to PNG for QA.

================================================================================
41. HUMAN REVIEW SCRIPT
================================================================================

A human reviewer should go page by page in this order:

Page 1:
    Does the second title line say Integraciones?
    Does it use the exact wide Helvetica-Bold shape?
    Is the background dark enough?
    Are the phones not squeezed?

Page 2:
    Are the three stat labels centered?
    Does the Enfoque block fit?
    Does the title use Helvetica-Bold, not condensed?

Page 3:
    Is the top-right screenshot framed?
    Are the bottom screenshots frameless?
    Are the five bottom screenshots centered as one group?
    Is the background consistent with page 1?

Page 4:
    Is the right title Nosotros desarrollamos?
    Do the two responsibility cards align?

Page 5:
    Is Integración productiva piloto in Incluye?
    Does the timeline breathe?

Page 6:
    Does title say Cómo arrancar?
    Is the intro fully visible?
    Is Primeros pasos compact and not overly spaced?

================================================================================
42. CLOSING NOTE FOR FUTURE IMPLEMENTERS
================================================================================

This brochure is small, but it is sensitive to typography and proportion. The
most important visual identity decisions are: Helvetica typography, dark damp
background, full-opacity screenshots, uncompressed phone assets, and disciplined
card spacing. Treat those as brand rules, not preferences.

END OF V9 EXPANSION.


================================================================================

================================================================================
44. FINAL REDUNDANT LOCK SUMMARY
================================================================================

This is the last short checklist repeated one more time because these are the
bugs most likely to regress:
- Use Helvetica / Helvetica-Bold only.
- Make the background dark, damp, deep navy, and unblurred.
- Keep screenshots 100% opaque and never horizontally compressed.
- Page 1 says Integraciones.
- Page 6 says Cómo arrancar.
- Page 3 bottom thumbnails are frameless and centered.
- Every page is 480 x 720 pt.
- The output is a six-page PDF, not images pasted into mismatched pages.

END OF STANDALONE V10 MANUAL.
